2026 年 被 動 元 件 呈 現 結 構 性 的 變 化 
AI 算力 升級重塑被動元件產業價 值鏈與評價體系  
⚫ AI 驅動 下被動元件產業由週期循 環轉向結構性成長  
⚫ 日韓大廠產能逼近滿載，產能稀缺性主導全球定價權  
⚫ 被動元件進入高單價成長與評價 re-rating 階段

AI 驅動下被動元件產業由週期循環轉向結構性成長 : 綜觀 2018
年 至 今 被 動元 件 產 業循環 演 變 ， 可明 顯 觀 察到產 業 驅 動 因子 已 由
過去以消費性電子為主的週期性景氣循環 ， 逐步轉型為由 AI 與高
階運算基礎建設所主導的結構性升級循環。 在 AI Server 架構下，
由 於 運 算 密度 、 功 耗與高 速 傳 輸 需求 大 幅 提升， 電 源 穩 定性 與 訊
號完整性要求同步升級，進一步推動高容值、高耐壓、低 ESR 與
高可靠度 MLCC 及相 關被動元件用量顯著增加。此一變化不僅帶
動 單 機 用 量 倍 數 成 長 ， 同 時 亦 推 升 產 品 平 均 單 價(ASP) 結構性上
移。以 Vera Rubin 平台為例，隨著系統進一步邁向更高功耗與更
高 算 力 密 度架 構 ， 整體電 源 設 計 與零 組 件 配置複 雜 度 顯 著提 升 ，
帶動單台 AI Server 所需被動元件價值相較前一代平台約提升 4~6
倍。  
日韓大廠產能逼近滿載，產能稀缺性主導全球定價權 : 日 韓 大 廠
(村田、三星、太陽誘電等 )產能近乎滿載，交期結構性拉長至 20 週
以上，並連動調漲報價 15% ～35% ，確立走向賣方市場。我們認為
供給端失衡短期內難以緩解 ， 核心瓶頸在於關鍵前段設備交期已拉
長至一年以上，且高容值、多層數產品涉及奈米級材料配方與高難
度燒結製程，導致有效產能釋放極度遲滯。在準時供應遠重於成本
控管的採購思維下，下游客戶為了防範斷料停工，展現高度價格彈
性，傾向透過支付風險溢價或簽署 LTA 長約鎖定產能。因此，即便
上游部分基礎原物料價格些微回落，常態性降價慣例亦將失效，報
價具備極強的向下剛性 ， 頭部廠商的毛利率與利潤結構將獲得強大
支撐。  
被動元件進入高單價成長與評價 re-rating 階段 : 2026 年被動元件
產業已轉向由 AI 伺服器與 1.6T 高速網通所驅動的結構性升級循
環。因系統功耗提升與高速傳輸門檻上升所帶動的規格升級，使產
品由標準化走向高階客製化，單機 BOM 價值持續上修，產業亦由
週期性轉為結構性成長。在此背景下，市場評價邏輯有望由低倍數
循環股，逐步轉向反映成長性與產品升級的 re-rating 過程。看好具
備 AI 與高階網通供應鏈切入能力之台廠，如國巨 (2327) 、華新科
(2492)、禾伸堂 (3026) 等，將優先受惠於本輪結構性升級循環與評價
重估行情。

被動元件三大核心產品 -電容 (C) 、電阻 (R) 、電感 (I) : 
被動元件 (Passive Components )為 電 子 產 品中 不可 或 缺 的基 礎零 組 件，主 要 功
能包括儲能 、濾波、穩壓、訊號傳輸與電流控制，廣泛應用於智慧型手機、 PC、
伺服器、車用電子、工業控制與通訊設備等領域。相較於半導體主動元件，被
動元件雖不具放大與運算功能 ， 但其用量龐大 ， 且隨電子產品規格升級 ， 單機
搭載量持續提升，因此具備高度產業連動性。  
目前被動元件產業主要可分為電容、電感與電阻三大核心產品，其中又以
MLCC (積層陶瓷電容 )為市場規模最大、技術門檻最高的產品，亦為目前 AI 伺
服器與車用電子升級下最受市場關注的領域。  
⚫ 電容 (Capacitor) ：儲能與 穩壓電容的主要功能在於儲存電能、濾除訊號雜
訊與穩定電壓。  
(1)  積層陶瓷電容 (MLCC) ：其具備體積小、電容量大、高頻特性佳等優
勢，極其適合表面黏著技術 (SMT) 。目前廣泛應用於智慧型手機、高
速運算 (HPC) 伺服器與汽車電子。近年隨 AI 伺服器、高速 GPU 與車
用電子規格升級， MLCC 需求明顯提升，尤其高容值、小尺寸、高
耐壓產品成為市場主流方向。  
(2)  鋁 質 電解 電 容： 體積 較大 ， 但具 備 極高 的單 位體 積 電容 量 且具 備 成
本優勢。主要應用於大功率、大電流的場景，如電源供應器、工業自
動化設備與家電。  
⚫ 電阻 (Resistor) ：主要 用 於調節與限制電路中的電流，並進行電壓分壓，其
中以晶片電阻 (Chip Resistor) 為目前主流產品。  
(1)  晶片電阻 (Chip Resistor) ： 為目前市場主流，體積小且量產規格標準
化 。 幾乎 所 有電 子產 品皆 須 大量 使 用晶 片電 阻， 以 達到 精 確的 阻 值
控制與電路保護。  
⚫ 電感 (Inductor) ：主要用 於抑制電流突變、穩定電流並過濾電磁干擾，在電
源管理與高速傳輸系統中扮演重要角色。  
(1)  磁珠 (Ferrite Bead): 主要 功能為吸收高頻雜訊並將其轉化為熱能，是
電 路 板 上 解 決 電 磁 相 容 性(EMC) 的 重 要 微 型 元 件 ， 廣 泛 應 用 於 智 慧
型手機、 PC、伺服器與通訊設備。  
(2)  功率電感 (Power Inductor): 主要應用於電源模組 (VRM) 與 DC-DC 轉
換系統，負責穩定電流與電壓。  
被動元件產業景氣循環， 2026 年結構性真質變 : 
被動元件產業屬於典型的電子零組件循環產業，景氣循環通常受到終端需求、
庫存水位、產能利用率、原材料價格與供應鏈結構變化影響。 2018 年至 2026 年
間，被動元件產業大致經歷四個主要循環階段，包括 2018 年全面性缺貨漲價、
2019 年庫存修正、 2020~2021 年疫情需求爆發及 2022~2024 年長時間去庫存，
直到 2026 年在 AI 與車用需求帶動下，再度進入新一輪高階化循環。

⚫ 第一循環： 2018 ～2021 年 
(1)  2018 年(缺貨期 )：由於日系大廠 (如 Murata 、TDK )策略性淡出 常規產
能、全力轉向車用領域，導致標準品 (如中低階 MLCC 、電阻 )供給出
現嚴重缺口 +智慧型手機規格升級與虛擬貨幣挖礦潮爆發， 在供需失
衡下， MLCC 價格出現大幅上漲，交期顯著拉長，通路與 EMS 端亦
出現明顯備貨與庫存堆積行為，進一步強化短期缺貨行情。各家大
廠產能利用率逼近滿載，產品報價與製造商毛利率創下歷史新高  
(2)  2019 年(景氣反轉與 去庫存期 )：2019 年產業迅速由高檔反轉，進入
典型庫存修正循環，主因在於 2018 年上半年過度備貨，使通路與終
端客戶庫存明顯偏高，隨著終端需求放緩及中美貿易戰影響擴大，
市場開始進行庫存去化，產品價格暴跌，產能利用率滑落至 40% ～
50% 的低谷。  
(3)  2020 ~2021 年(疫情初期的 紅利期 )：2020 年初新冠疫情爆發，遠距辦
公與教學帶動 PC、筆電及平板需求迎來爆發式增長，庫存水位迅速降
至健康線以下，價格止跌回升。  
⚫ 第二循環： 2022~2023 年 
(1)  2022 ～2023 年(消費性產品需求下滑 ): 隨著各國全面解封、全球通膨
飆升，以及消費性電子需求明顯疲弱，使智慧型手機與 PC 市場進入
庫存調整週期，整體 產業進入長時間下行修正循環。此階段產業結構
開 始出 現分 化， 高階產品 (車用 、工 控、伺 服器 )需 求相 對穩 定，而 標
準型產品則面臨供過於求壓力，使產業開始由單一循環轉向雙軌結
構。  
⚫ 第三循環： 2025~2026 年 
(1)  2025~2026 年(被動元件產業進入新一輪循環 ): 本輪驅動已明顯不同
於過往以消費性電子為主的景氣週期，轉而由 AI 伺服器與高速運算
基 礎 建 設 成 為 核 心 動 能 ， 產 業 需 求 轉 為 高 階 規 格 集 中 成 長 。 隨AI 
Server 快速擴張，高功率密度與高速運算帶動電源規格升級，高容值、
高耐壓與低損耗 MLCC 需求顯著提升 ， 同時亦推動功率電感與電源相
關元件同步成長 。AI 伺服器單機 被動元件 用量約為傳統伺服器的 2 至
4 倍，使整體需求結構出現明顯跳升。

同時，我們觀察到 Murata 等日韓系大廠持續聚焦高階與車用應用 ， 減
少中低階產能配置 ， 使高階 MLCC 供給長期偏緊，市場呈現高階產品
緊缺的狀態。另外，貴金屬價格上漲亦推升成本，使高階產品具備更
佳價格轉嫁能力。在供需雙重推動下，目前日韓大廠優先配置 AI 高
階產能，產生排擠效應，使中低階訂單外溢至台廠，如國巨 (2327) 、華
新科 (2492) 等，帶動台系廠商同步受惠於量價齊揚。

驅動結構性的 三大因素 -AI Server 、網通與 車用  
(1)  AI Server  
隨著 AI 運算時代由 NVIDIA Hopper 架構走向 Vera Rubin 平台以及各大雲
端服務供應商 (CSP) 積極開出客製化高階 ASIC 專案， AI 伺服器中的被動
元件 (電感、電容、電阻 )正全面啟動跨世代的結構性升級 。 以整櫃被動元件
產值觀察，傳統伺服器機櫃的被動元件價值量約僅 1,000~1,500 美元；然
而隨  NVIDIA GB200 ／GB300 NVL72 架構導入高功率 GPU 、HBM 、高速
交換器與液冷系統後，整櫃被動元件產值已提升至約 2,500~3,500 美元；
展望 Vera Rubin 平台，由於 GPU 功耗、 HBM4 頻寬與機櫃供電規格再度
大幅升級，市場預估整櫃被動元件產值將正式突破 5,000~6,500 美元。我
們認為 受惠幅度最大的產品，將集中於：  
⚫ 機櫃級高電流電源架構所需之高階功率電感 (Power Inductor)  
AI 晶片在進行超 大型語 言模型 (LLM) 訓 練或複 雜推論時， 動態負 載切
換 劇 烈 ， 瞬 間 電 流 可 高 達 數 千 安 培 。 為 了 避 免GPU 因 瞬 間 電 壓 驟 降
(Vdroop) 導致系統當機或斷電，供電模組 (VRM) 必須具備極快的暫態響
應速度 (Transient Response) ，因此需導入更高效率、更低損耗的特規功
率電感，包括大電流一體成型電感 (Molding Choke) 以 及 新 一 代
TLVR( Trans -Inductor Voltage Regulator) 雙繞組轉流電感。  
⚫ GPU ／HBM 周邊所需之小尺寸、高容值 (High -CV) MLCC  
AI 晶 片 與 高 速 記 憶 體(HBM) 的 運 行 頻 率 極 高 ， 且 基 板(Substrate) 上的
佈線空間極其有限 (Real Estate 限制 )，需要在極小空間內發揮最大的去
耦(Decoupling) 與濾波功能，以消除高頻雜訊並穩定電壓。因此 MLCC 
必須朝 0201 、01005 等微型化發展，同時必須滿足超高容值 (High -CV，
如 0201 尺寸達到 1uF~2.2uF 以上 )與高耐溫 (125°C~150°C) 的嚴苛條件 。

⚫ 高壓直流 (HVDC) 驅動大尺寸高壓 MLCC 、高階鉭質電容 與特規電容  
隨著 Rubin 機櫃總功耗持續提升，目前高階 AI Rack 功耗已普遍超過  
100kW ，傳統的 12V 甚至 48V 輸電架構在傳輸如此龐大的電量時，會
產生高昂的線路損耗。因此部分美系 CSP 已開始導入獨立 HVDC 電力
機櫃，市場預期 2027 年~2028 年將加速邁向 800V HVDC 架構。  
在高壓、高溫與高功率密度環境下，電力端與高階 DC-DC 主功率級將
大量採用 1206、1210 以上大尺寸 X7R/X7S 等高階高壓 MLCC，並大量
複 合 高 可 靠 度 鉭 質 電 容 與 導 電 高 分 子 電 容 ， 以 滿 足 大 容 量 濾 波 與 高 耐
壓需求 。同時 ，隨著主機板信號傳輸速率飙升與精密電源管理 (PMIC )的
精準度要求，高頻、超高穩定度 (Ultra -stabl e) 的 NP0 (COG ) MLCC 在控
制迴路與高頻雜訊抑制中的技術門檻與用量亦將同步拉高。  
(2)  網通  
網通設備由 800G 升級至 1.6T 世代，整體對訊號完整性要求大幅提高，使
傳統標準型 MLCC 已難以完全滿足高頻低損耗需求，進一步推升高階客製
化電容產品導入；在供電端方面， AI 交換器晶片功耗持續攀升且電流變動
劇烈，使系統需配置更多高容量與高反應速度電容，同時電感產品亦因新
一代供電架構導入而出現用量與單價同步提升；此外，在封裝技術朝向  
CPO 發展下，被動元件進一步朝微型化與高耐熱製程演進，部分產品甚至
逐步跨入半導體級製程門檻，整體技術門檻明顯提高。  
(3)  車用  
電動車的動力電池與電氣系統正全面從傳統的 400V 電壓平台 ， 向800V 高
壓平台演進。當電壓提升至 800V ，車載充電器 (OBC) 、逆變器 (Inverter) 和
直流轉換器 (DC -DC Converter) 在開關切換時，會產生極其劇烈的高壓浪湧
(Voltage Surge) 與嚴重的 電磁干擾 (EMI)。電動化帶來高壓大功率元件 (薄膜
電容、高壓 MLCC 、大功率電感 )，智能化帶來了高算力周邊的去耦與高頻
濾波元件 (低 ESL 電容、精密電阻 )。兩者疊加，讓單車被動元件的產值呈
現數倍的結構性擴張。  
圖表三、電動車 400V 到 800V

202 6 /05 /29

7 
高階產品擴充速度慢，導致交期延長  
目前全球主要日韓被動元件廠商已陸續反映高階產品供需失衡壓力，且市場結
構正由過去相對平衡，逐步轉向由供給端主導的賣方市場 (Seller’s Market) 。全
球 MLCC 龍頭 Murata 目前高階產線稼動率已逼近 95% 滿載。公司於今年 2 月
已針對 AI 伺服器專用高容  MLCC 、高階車規產品及 RF 元件調升報價約 15%
～35%，同時高階產品交期亦由過往約 4 週，結構性拉長至 20 週以上。另一方
面，Taiyo 亦同步調漲高階 MLCC、一體成型電感與鋁電容等產品價格，並持續
將資本支出 (CAPEX) 與新增產能優先投入 AI、車用與高階工控領域，顯示日系
廠商整體策略已明顯朝高毛利、高技術門檻產品集中，而非擴充中低階常規品
產能。  
韓系龍頭三星電機則率先於代理商與現貨市場反映價格調整 ， 高階 MLCC 現貨
價格調幅已超過 20% 。隨著日韓主要供應商同步調升報價、控管交期與優先配
置高階客戶產能，全球高階被動元件市場已逐步形成結構性的賣方市場。  
我們認為本輪高階被動元件供需失衡短期內難以快速緩解，主因在於全球主要
供應商近年持續將有限的資本支出、設備與研發資源優先投入 AI、車用與高階
工控產品，而非擴充中低階常規品產能。換言之，本輪產業循環已不同於過往
由消費性電子需求驅動的全面性景氣復甦，而是由 AI 與高階運算需求所帶動
的結構性升級週期。此外，高階 MLCC 的擴產難度亦顯著高於一般產品。上游
關鍵設備，包括高精密疊層塗佈機、高溫共燒爐與高階檢測設備等，交期普遍
已拉長至一年以上；同時，高容值、高層數 MLCC 涉及奈米級材料配方、超薄
介電層技術、高階燒結製程與長時間客戶驗證，導致有效產能釋放速度受限，
短期內難以透過快速擴產解決供給缺口。  
基於全球高階被動元件面臨產能結構性瓶頸，我們預期若此供需缺口持續存在
並進一步放大，為了避免因單一核心元件缺料而導致高客單價的 AI 伺服器或
車用模組延遲出貨，客戶端 有望 溢價 (Risk Premium )購買 或提供長期合約 (LTA )
來提前鎖定高階被動元件的產能。因此我們認為即便未來上游部分基礎原物料
(如銅、鎳、白銀等 )價格出現些微回落，被動元件廠也不會面臨客戶端常態性的
降價 (Cost -down )要求。在產能稀缺性主導的賣方市場格局下，高階被動元件的
報價將具備極強的向下剛性 (Price Downward Rigidity )，短期之內價格要向下調
整的機率低，頭部廠商的毛利率與利潤結構將獲得強大支撐。  
2026 年被動元件迎來結構性的變化，評價面有望 re-rate  
我們重申，相較 2018 年以手機與消費性電子復甦為主、需求分散且高度週期化
的行情， 2026 年被動元件產業已明顯轉向由 AI 伺服器與 1.6T 高速網通所驅動
的結構性升級循環。本輪成長核心不再來自出貨量擴張，而是源自系統功耗提
升與高速傳輸門檻上升所帶動的規格升級，使被動元件由標準化零組件轉向高
階客製化與高單價產品結構，帶動單機 BOM 價值持續上修，產業成長動能因
此更具延續性與可預期性。在此背景下，被動元件產業本質已由過往的景氣循
環產業，逐步轉型為由技術升級與產品組合優化所驅動的成長型產業。  
因此，我們認為市場對產業的評價邏輯將出現結構性轉折，從過去以景氣循環
為基礎的低倍數評價，逐步轉向反映結構性成長與產品升級紅利的 re-rating 過
程。具備 AI 與高階網通滲透能力的廠商，將率先受惠於此一評價重估循環。

公司拜訪快報

裕隆(2201) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：透過產線多樣化，爭取更多代工機會

一、透過產線多樣化，爭取更多代工機會 
台灣 2025 年新車銷售 41.4 萬輛，YoY -9%，主要為受
到進口關稅不確定壓抑需求，4Q25 在政府貨物稅優惠
政策延續下，銷售有回升。 
1~3 月新車銷售短期仍受關稅影響下滑 3%， 目前在貨物
稅政策延續與關稅問題影響放緩下，公司預期 2026 年
新車銷售較去年持平或小幅成長。而市場龍頭和泰車，
預期 2026 年新車銷售 44 萬輛，YoY 6.2%。 
裕隆透過產線多樣化與鴻華開發多款原型車，爭取更多
代工機會，目前 Foxtron Bria 與 MG 的 MPV 車代工已
順利量產。 
國內車市概況

二、納智捷售出，裕隆專注於生產代工 
鴻華先進以 7.876 億元收購裕隆子公司納智捷汽車全數
股權，取得納智捷的主導經營權。從「研發設計」領域
拓展至 「品牌與銷售」 ，的電動車一條龍服務。未來鴻華
先進除認列技術授權或銷售底盤的收入，將能認列整車
銷售的營收，擴大營運規模，不過初期因併購營運費用
增加，與未來銷售與品牌經營將更具挑戰。 
而對裕隆而言，納智捷的股權移轉案已於 2026 年 4 
月 10 日完成相關交割作業，自今年第二季起，納智捷
將不再列入裕隆的合併報表。以 2025 年數據推估，今
年將影響營收近 40 億元 。 不過納智捷過去的品牌營運與
行銷費用是不小的負擔，出售後，將可擺脫品牌經營風
險與財務壓力，公司專注於生產代工，有利母公司財務
改善。未來鴻華先進將以新品牌 Foxtron 推出新車款。
目前已推出 Model B 量產版的 Bria，第二款電動休旅
Cavira 將為繼 n7 後推出的車款，於 5/20 發表並開始預
接訂單，將於 6/17 正式上市。 
此外，鴻華先進 2Q25 與三菱簽署合作備忘錄，開發右
駕電動車，布局海外市場，預計 2026 年下半年進軍紐
澳市場，並委由裕隆代工於台灣生產，也將挹注未來成
長動能。 
三、預期 2026 年 EPS 1.5 元 
裕隆 1Q26 營收 169.75 億元 ，-8.3%QoQ，-3.5%YoY，
主要為整體車市仍在調整及金融服務收益減少。毛利率
與營業利益受惠成本結構優化以及費用控制得宜，優於
預期，不過業外受轉投資公司仍在調整影響，單季 EPS 
0.32 元與預估相當。 
展望 2026 年，鴻華先進推出款兩新車款，目前集團旗
下裕日車轉投資東風日產，與中華車陸續引進新車型，
有助於挹注營運動能，加上政府推出貨物稅減徵，預期
2026 年營收 704.44 億元 ，YoY -2.6%。自 2Q26 納智捷
品牌出售後，未來專注代工，將有利毛利率轉好，推估
EPS 1.5 元。 
四、 「整車製造」及「能源發展」雙核心方向發展  
公司除了持續拓展汽車代工業務，並啟動動力電池量產
製造。集團擁有研發工業型儲能機櫃及電池管理系統能
力，推動儲能產品，提升能源生產規模。未來朝「整車
製造」及「能源發展」雙核心方向發展。 
新店裕隆城其主體建物樓層為 B3~8F，約4.3 萬坪 ，B1F
～4F 為誠品商場 、5F~6F 為商場 、7F～8F 為威秀影廳。
目前來店人次穩定增加，每年可貢獻組金收入 5 億元。

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部維持對偉詮電的買進投資建議，係因(1)1Q26 本業獲利大
幅提升且優於預期，(2)2Q26 具有上修動能，(3)雖然 AI SERVER
走向液冷，但新增 POWER RACK 風扇需求，且 FAN DRIVER 單價將
提升，(4)上修 26 年營收及獲利預估。綜合以上，預估偉詮電26
年 EPS 達到 4.51 元(原估 3.89 元，+64%YOY)，雖然1Q26 因為業
外獲利較少使 EPS 不如預期，不過本業成長動能躍升，證明其產
品競爭力及技術，故給予買進建議，評價給予26 年的 20 倍本益
比，目標價 90 元。

重點摘要：

1. 1Q26 本業獲利優於預期： 
偉詮電 1Q26 營收為 創 歷 史新 高的 10.47 億元，成 長
13.2%QOQ，成長 31.7%YOY，其中主要是 SERVER 風扇馬達
驅動 IC 出貨持續增長 ， 使得1Q26 智慧應用佔比從 4Q25 的
36%提升至 1Q26 的 43%， 而此產線毛利率較高 ， 也使得1Q26
偉詮電毛利率提升至 33.5%， 優於原先預期的32.3%， 整體
營業利益跳升至 1.42 億元，大幅優於先前預估的 8200 萬
元，OPM 也跳升至 13.6%。業外利益僅有 3100 萬元，相對
於 4Q25 大幅降低 ， 係因大盤在三月底較動盪 ， 轉投資評價
利益較少 。 使得稅後淨利僅為1.38 億元(原估 1.92 億元)，
衰退 53.3%QOQ，成長 466.6%YOY，EPS 0.7 元(原估 0.96
元)， 但就營業利益來看則是大幅優於原先預期約35-40%。

2. 2Q26 具上修動能： 
偉詮電已經公告 4 月營收為 3.9 億元，創歷史同期新高，
主要仍是 SERVER 風扇相關 IC 拉貨動能受到 AI SERVER 需
求帶動 ， 需求增長幅度持續擴大 ， 故研究部上修2Q26 營收
預估為 11.93 億元(原估 10.78 億元)，成長14%QQO，成長
28.6%YOY，另外因為智慧相關應用佔比持續提升，推估毛
利率將提升至 34.4%(原估 33.1%)，稅後淨利預估同步上
修 2.72 億元(原估 2.15 億元)，成長 98.5%QOQ，成長
988%YOY，EPS 1.36 元(原估 1.07 元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. 走向液冷，但風扇在散熱產值中仍持續增長： 
公司在法說中表示 ， 雖然目前AI SERVER散熱走向液冷 ， 但
多數仍是L2A， 故風扇整櫃用量約是350個 ， 進入到VR系列 ，
會開始走向L2L規格，單櫃風扇會減少，但是會出現POWER 
RACK，一櫃COMPUTE RACK搭配2櫃的POWER RACK，而一櫃的
POWER RACK將會使用72個風扇 ， 另外因為走48V， 相對於先
前12V，單顆FAN IC 將會是先前的一倍以上，故走向VR系
列 ， 就算L2L都未用風扇貨或是水泵下 ， 整體FAN IC產值將
不會減少。先前認為走向VR系列，將會走向液冷，故下半
年FAN IC成長幅度將趨緩，但因為FAN IC單價將提升，且
有POWER RACK風扇挹注下，上修26年智慧相關IC產線營收
成長70-80%YOY，原估50%YOY。

4. 產品組合優化狀況優於預期，上修毛利率預估 
偉詮電主要有三大產品，分別為 POWER 相關(USB PD 及
SWITHCING)、智慧應用(FAN DRIVER)及代理羅姆半導體產
品(分離式元件)，毛利率區間分別為，POWER約略低於平
均，FAN DRIVER高於平均，而代理產品毛利率約11-13%。
因為FAN DRIVER需求優於預期，成長性比原先預估更高，
加上羅姆半導體的分離式元件有所漲價，故將抵銷晶圓代
工及封測的漲價，進而帶動毛利率的增長，上修26年毛利
率預估至34.7%(原估33.3%)。

5. 本業動能優於預期，氣冷散熱有延續性 
1Q26偉詮電受惠到客戶對FAN DRIVER拉貨動能強勁，使得
本業獲利優於預期，雖然業外獲利不如前季，但從本業可
以看出產品組合優化對於營業利益的大幅帶動。展望未
來，2Q26將持續上修，且雖然VR系列走向液冷，但在出現
POWER RACK風冷需求，加上FAN DRIVER因為電壓值提高而
使單價提升，相對先前對於風冷較為悲觀的想法有所扭
轉，故上修26年偉詮電營收為46.5億元(原估42.3億元)，
成長30.3%YOY ，稅後淨利預估為 9.01 億元(原估7.78億
元)，成長64%YOY，EPS 4.51元(原估3.89元)。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

Price Target NT$121.00 NT$160.00
WPG reported strong 1Q earnings:    WPG reported its 1Q results. The 1Q revenue
was NT$316.5bn, +24%     Q/Q, +27% Y/Y, in line with MSe. Revenue was driven by AI
infra     build-out and semiconductors demand, and expanded supply chain     services

(e.g., LaaS). 1Q gross profit was NT$14.2bn, gross margin was 4.5%, +0.2ppt Q/Q and
+0.8ppt Y/Y, 0.2ppt above our forecast. 1Q operating profit was NT$8.5bn, with
operating margin at 2.7%, +0.5ppt Q/Q and  +1.0ppt Y/Y, 0.2ppt above our estimate.
1Q (diluted)     EPS was NT$3.17, +95% Q/Q and +192% Y/Y, 12% above our estimate.
ROWC reached     15.2%, up from 11.5% in 4Q25. This follows the strong 1Q results of
WT Micro: Scale drives operating leverage

.
Strong 2Q guidance by WPG: The company guided for mid-point 2Q revenue of NT
$355bn, gross margin of     4.35%, operating margin of 2.58% and (non-diluted) EPS of
NT$3.86. The strong     outlook is driven by continued global AI demand and capex
guidance,     and management expects continued strong demand from inference and
edge AI.
Other highlights from WPG 1Q earnings
: By application type, compute,
communication, consumer, industrial and auto     accounted for 49%, 15%, 8%, 7% and

7%, respectively, which shows strong growth for     compute and moderate growth for
industrial and auto. By device type,     memory and core component accounted for 45%
and 23%, respectively. The reduction in     the core component mix from 31% in 4Q25
represents the company's     drive to improve margins.
The AI story lifts earnings for both WT and WPG:
Based on the actual 1Q results
for WPG and strong guidance for 2Q     forward, we raise our earnings estimates:

•  WT – EPS up 12%,  17% and 16% for 2026-28, respectively;
• WPG – EPS up 32% for each 2026-28.
We raise price targets for  WT from NT$299 to NT$349 and for      WPG from NT
$121 to NT$160:    We continue to use residual income valuation models; PT changes
reflect our earnings estimate revisions.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
WPG reported strong 1Q earnings with EPS 12% above MSe.
WPG 2Q mid-point guidance implies 12% Q/Q revenue growth and 17% Q/Q EPS
growth.
WPG management remains positive on the secular AI-driven demand.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: We met with Taiwan Surface Mounting Technology (TSMT)’s 
management on 21 May (see our 13 May flash). Per management, its 
product mix for 2025 was: TFT-LCD 46%, Mini LED 11%, DRAM module 
16%, auto 9%, cable 9%, touch 5%, and others 4%. Management guides 
for the mix for 2026 to be: TFT-LCD 46%, Mini LED 11%, DRAM module 
17%, auto 8%, cable 6%, touch 5%, and others 7%. The QFII holding of 
TSMT was 18.2% on 20 May vs 19.8% at the end of 2025.

Highlights: 2026-27 guidance. TSMT guides for YoY revenue growth of 5-
10% (close to the high end; +1.9% YoY revenue growth for 4M26) for 2026 
and a double-digit % YoY growth for 2027, thanks to the multiple revenue 
drivers from AI-related demand. For 2026, the company expects gross 
margin of 13-15% (up QoQ; vs 12.8% gross margin in 2025), with further 
YoY growth in 2027 on a more favourable product mix. Its AI-related 
revenue target is 15-20% for 2027, up from 5-6% in 4Q26 and 1-2% in 
2026. For 2026, the company expects 2H26 revenue to be higher than that 
in 1H26, thanks to the rising order outlook from DRAM modules and AI-
related revenue supported by its capacity ramping up from its Mexico, 
India, and Vietnam plants.

Multiple revenue drivers to kick off from 2H26. TSMT mentioned several 
revenue drivers as follows: 1) Optical transceiver assembly. TSMT will be 
the major supplier of 800G and 1.6T products to its US-based client, and 
the company has seen some outsourcing demand from China-based 
clients, with 4-5% of combined revenue contribution in 4Q26. 2) DRAM 
module. TSMT expects the revenue contribution to rise to 20-25% in 2H26, 
up from 18% in 2Q26, and to grow YoY in 2027 considering its US- and 
China-based clients. 3) Low earth orbit (LEO). TSMT will start delivering 
SMT products in 2H26 for a US-based client. 4) Humanoid robots. The 
company sees business opportunity (sensor modules and PCBA) from the 
US-based client with revenue contribution from 2027, likely to reach a low 
single-digit % of 2027 revenue. 5) Battery backup unit (BBU). The 
company will trial run products in 4Q26 or 2027. 6) Server SMT. Currently, 
TSMT has two clients and plans to add a new one in 2H26 from Taiwan or 
the US. Thus, the company guides for capex of USD100-150m over 2026-
27, up from USD100m in 2025, subject to its capacity expansion plan in 
Mexico, as it expects capacity constraints for SMT in the next 1-2 years 
due to the increasing use of AI applications. The company also plans to 
expand its production capacity in China, Vietnam, Taiwan, and Mexico.

Valuation: The stock is currently trading at PERs of 21.4x and 17.4x based 
on the Bloomberg consensus’ 2026E and 2027E EPS, respectively.

21 May 2026

Positive tone from management

➢ TSMT expects stronger YoY revenue growth for 2027 vs 2026 
➢ Several new drivers to underpin revenue outlook over 2026-27 
➢ AI-related revenue to reach 15-20% of 2027 revenue vs 1-2% for 2026

ABF Substrates – Takeaways
from AT&S FY25/26 Results Call
AT&S raised revenue and EBITDA margin guidance for FY26/27,
which supports our bullish view on ABF substrate suppliers
Unimicron, NYPCB, and ZDT.

Howard Kao

Sharon.Shih@morganstanley.com                                               +886 2 2730-2865
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Medium-term 2026/27 guidance
• Revenue: +30-35% y/y (implying the high end of the communicated €2.1-
2.4bn range)
• EBITDA margin: 25-29% (raised from 24-28% previously)
• *This guidance is subject to supply and the geopolitical situation, as well as
currency developments. Also not included are the potential effects of

ongoing negotiations with customers. These would result in a capacity
expansion that is partially financed by customers and would accordingly
impact earnings performance and capital expenditure planning.
Other key takeaways:
• (+) FY26/27 revenue growth will be mainly driven by volume growth, with
Microelectronics (ABF substrate) seeing more pronounced growth from AI
infra investments, which impact demand for both GPU and increasingly CPU
as the ecosystem broadens towards more inference workloads.

• (+) AT&S noted that customer demand for its technologies is so large that
some of them cannot be fulfilled; particularly for Microelectronics, it is
seeing strong demand from both existing and new customers.
• (+) Now AT&S' agreements with its customers often include financing for
investments from its customers, one example being the newly announced
capacity expansion for high-end substrates in Chongqing, China, for which the
required investments in the 'high double-digit € million' range will be fully

financed based on long-term customer agreements. This is expected to
ensure profitability for AT&S.
• (+) AT&S also noted increasing input costs from suppliers, which AT&S
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
AT&S (ATSV.VI, not covered) reported C1Q revenue of €477mn (+2% q/q):
Electronics Solutions (-13% q/q) and Microelectronics (ABF; +14% q/q).
C1Q/F4Q Microelectronics (ABF) EBIT margin jumped by 920bp q/q to 17.4%,
which management attributed to bigger volumes in all sites and pricing effects.
FY26/27 revenue was guided to grow 30-35% y/y, primarily driven by volumes,

with Microelectronics (ABF) seeing more pronounced growth...
...and EBITDA margin was guided to fall in the range of 25-29% (vs. 23.3% in
FY25/26), on the back of volumes, pricing and passing on increasing input costs.
May 21, 2026 04:11 PM GMT

expects to pass on to its customers, though typically with a delay of a few

• (+) AT&S confirmed that it did adjust the pricing for its ABF substrate
products in C1Q/F4Q.
• (=)  AT&S is also engaging with partners and customers to develop future
technologies, including larger substrates, glass core substrates, embedded

component substrates/PCBs, optical PCBs, etc.
Our thoughts: Management sounded very positive during the call, citing several
growth tailwinds for ABF substrates, including larger volumes, more customer
commitments and continuing pricing actions to offset inflationary cost pressures.
This echoes our view that we are just in the early phase of an upcycle, and that the
substrate suppliers will be able to expand margins through pricing adjustments that

more than offset the cost pressures. We believe AT&S' view on ABF substrates
aligns with our positive view on Unimicron, NYPCB, and Zhen Ding.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers
n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn&#39;t require substrate
(e.g., CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Zhen Ding (4958.TW)

Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 10%, a medium-
term growth rate of 15% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Higher-than-expected F-PCB content increase for iPhones
n Better-than-expected production yield/share allocation for SLP
n Share gains on higher margin F-PCB pieces
Risks to Downside
n Worse-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Lower-than-expected F-PCB content increase for iPhones

n Worse-than-expected production yield/share allocation for SLP
n Increasing competition from Chinese peers, intensifying pricing pressure
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)

[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Sharon.Shih@morganstanley.com                                               +886 2 2730-2865
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
At ISCAS 2026 in Shanghai on May 25, Huawei introduced the "t (Tau) Law" - a time-
based scaling principle for multi-layer electronic systems that aims to succeed
Moore's Law as geometric scaling faces limitations. This approach optimizes a
unified time constant (t) across all computing levels—from transistors to data

centers—shifting focus from size reduction to overall system efficiency, including
interconnects, signal integrity, power, and thermal management.
Under this new concept, Huawei has innovated "Logic Folding" — vertically stacking
active layers for digital, analog, and memory circuits, which has achieved a 55%
increase in transistor density and a 41% gain in power efficiency at a fixed process
node.
At the AI cluster level,  t Law, combined with technologies such as a unified memory

semantic bus interconnect architecture, near-package optical Hi-ONE (in-package
high-speed optoelectronic interconnects), and edge-to-surface 3D Folding, is
projected to achieve more than a 100× increase in hardware integration by 2035.
Global AI Transceivers: Industry Demand Likely to be Even Stronger (14 May 2026)
Global Technology: AI Transceivers: Growth Dominates Disruption (26 Feb 2026)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We believe the "t (Tau) Law" is a fundamental theory supporting the exponential
growth of the AI transceiver industry. We reiterate our positive view.
t (Tau) Law is a  framework introduced by Huawei to optimize signal delay (t) to
boost transistor density, energy efficiency, and performance.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We attended SDI’s investor conference on 27 May. We now 
foresee a brighter-than-expected revenue and gross-margin outlook over 
2026, thanks to its better-than-expected auto, industrial, and consumer 
electronic segments along with the upside potential for its AI lead frame 
and heat spreader as well as multiple price hikes (due to cost inflation and 
a copper price hike YTD). We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our latest flash 
1Q26 results: a strong beat, 7 May).

What’s the impact: Brighter-than-expected lead frame outlook. For 
2Q26, SDI expects its revenue to grow double-digit % QoQ in addition to a 
better order outlook from auto (recovering revenue momentum from 
existing products and rising revenue contribution from new products) and 
industrial (improving revenue momentum since 2Q26), and better-than-
feared consumer revenue with rising momentum in the recent three 
months. SDI mentioned that the utilisation rate is high at its plants in China 
(from auto demand with new products contribution) and Taiwan (from AI, 
auto, and industrial demand for IDM and OSAT clients).

Rising revenue contribution from AI lead frame and heat spreader. Its 
AI lead frame accounted for 6% of revenue in 1Q26, and management 
targets to reach double-digit revenue contribution by 4Q26. SDI highlights 
that the company has received many rush orders for AI lead frame, and 50-
60 new AI-related new products are expected to be launched in 2H26. SDI 
is obtaining product approval for its heat spreader and expects “tens of 
millions” of TWD contribution in 2026, mainly from auto and the CPO 
switch. SDI is currently testing the production for CPU and GPU. Although 
less than 1% of revenue contribution is expected in 2026, SDI is positive 
about its heat spreader outlook going forward. In terms of gross margin, 
SDI will likely optimise its product mix and hike prices in 2Q26. It is 
planning another price hike for 2H26. We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 14-
19%, mainly due to our more aggressive gross margin assumption on a 
better product mix, rising automation level, and production efficiency.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating with a new 12M TP 
of TWD265 (from TWD192), now based on a target PER of 39x (from 30x), 
still at the lower end of its past-5-year range of 12-73x [average PER: 28x]) 
due to its strong earnings visibility (net profit CAGR of 27.0% over 2026-
28E, from 15.6% previously) on our 1-year forward EPS. Key downside 
risks: worse-than-expected end demand and worse-than-expected gross-
margin expansion.

How we differ: Our 2026E EPS is 7% higher than the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more aggressive view on its revenue and 
gross-margin expansion.

27 May 2026

Stronger-than-expected order visibility

➢ Ramping up AI lead frames revenue with rush orders 
➢ Heat-spreader products started to contribute revenue in 2026 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12M TP to TWD265

Company profile 
Founded in 1967, SDI started out as a stationery producer and set up its electronics department in 
1983. SDI is currently the No.1 power lead frame manufacturer in the world, whose major segments are 
automotive (42% of revenue in 2024), industrial (18%), consumer (24%), stationery (13%) and others 
(2%). Its electrical business accounted for 87% of total revenue in 2025. The company was listed on 
Taiwan’s main board in April 1996.

What’s Changed
AllRing Tech Co. (6187.TWO) From To
Price Target NT$1,280.00 NT$1,580.00
Computex focus: Rubin/Vera rack designs and thermal/package
updates. We see stronger TSMC CoWoS expansion. We remain

positive on the NVDA supply chain, and raise our PT for AllRing.
Computex runs June 2–5, with the NVIDIA CEO keynote set for June 1. We expect
demos of AI server and networking system designs, not limited to NVIDIA. Key focus:
1. Rubin series sever rack designs: We look forward to the combination of
NVL (GPU rack Kyber with CPO scale-up connection), LPU rack, Vera CPU
rack, BlueField rack, and NVSwitch rack - together targeting the lowest cost
per token for a future AI Factory. We will see if AMD demonstrates Helios

racks sharing token output data.
2. The US$20bn NVIDIA CPU opportunity: Our US semi analyst Joe Moore
expects NVIDIA to provide more details. Our supply chain checks suggest
TSMC is allocating additional CoWoS-R capacity and 3nm wafers for the
Vera CPU. Based on capacity, we think 1.5mn units of standalone CPUs is a
reasonable assumption. End customers could be Microsoft, Meta,
CoreWeave, and Oracle.
3. What about the Rubin schedule and Rubin Ultra package design?

Investors wonder whether Rubin GPU can achieve its target TDP of 2.3kW.
There is also some doubt that Rubin Ultra will be 4-die per package, as
warpage issue still encountered. Our understanding is that Rubin was rolled
back to the original chip thermal solution and achieved 1.8kW TDP; through
server-system thermal collaboration, the 2.3kW TDP can still be achieved.
For Rubin Ultra, NVIDIA needs new photomasks to adopt HBM4e in 2027,
and it should stick with 2-die per package to maintain good chip yield.

We lift our TSMC 2027 CoWoS capacity assumption to 200kwpm from
170kwpm, and slightly trim our SoIC capacity build forecasts: Very strong AI GPU
and CPU demand led TSMC to further expand CoWoS capacity in AP7.  Our checks
suggest TSMC is turning its Fab 15A 28nm/22nm space to 55nm interposer
production. Hence UMC should receive order overflow from Sony’s 28/22nm ISP in
2027; we remain OW on UMC. For SoIC, we still see production unchanged at
30/60kwpm in 2027/2028, while production build might be slightly delayed for

three quarters; we therefore trim our capacity build assumptions to 40/70kwpm for
2027/2028, from 45/78kwpm, respectively. We remain OW on NVIDIA for its key
supply chain for Rubin and Vera, including KYEC, ASE, Hon Precision and  Winway, let
alone TSMC in foundry. We lift AllRing’s price target to NT$1,580, from NT$1,280,
reflecting strong CoWoS capacity build, especially in 2027.

2
Global CoWoS Capacity Update
CoWoS capacity – update on capacity build
Driven by very strong demand coming from AI GPU and CPU and potential rising
competition from other 2.5D packaging solutions, TSMC has decided to further expand its
CoWoS capacity  in AP7 at the expense of SoIC’s capacity expansion, which is less urgent.
We revise our 2027 CoWoS capacity estimate from 160-170kwpm to 200kwpm. TSMC has
confirmed that it is turning its Fab 15A 28nm/22nm space to 55nm interposer production

by adding around 10kwpm, which is further new expansion this year after just building
15kwpm of interposer capacity in Singapore and filling all interposer capacity in Fab 12 and
Fab 14. Hence, we think UMC could receive the order overflow from Sony’s 28nm/22nm
ISP in 2027, and we reiterate OW on UMC.
For SoIC, we still expect production of 30kwpm for 2027 and 60kwpm for 2028. The
production build might be slightly delayed for 3 quarters, and hence we trim our capacity

build assumptions from  45kwpm in 2027 and 78kwpm in 2028 to 40kwpm and 70kwpm,
respectively.

SoIC capacity and demand breakdown
Current customers adopting SoIC include:
• NVIDIA (covered by Joseph Moore): We expect annual SoIC consumption of 6k in
2026, rising to 120k in 2027. This includes demand for CPO's COUPE. We see more
clues from the Asian supply chain that NVIDIA's next-generation GPU Feynman
could require 1-2GB of SRAM stacked on each die within the Feynman package. On
the other hand, we also see NVIDIA studying GPU-to-GPU stacking, which should

translate to SoIC demand.
• AMD (covered by Joseph Moore): AMD has adopted SoIC on its AI and gaming
GPU and high-end CPU, and we  do not rule out future adoption for its ASIC
business. We  expect AMD's SoIC consumption at 42k in 2026, increasing to 60k in
2027.
• Apple (covered by Erik Woodring): Apple's data center and Mac Pro chips rely on
SoIC. We expect Apple's SoIC consumption at 36k in 2026, increasing to 60k in
2027.
• Others: Several customers are now trying TSMC's SoIC capacity, including

Broadcom, Qualcomm (both covered by Joseph Moore), and AWS. We think this is
in line with our understanding that Broadcom would start to introduce SoIC
packaging for its networking chips and ASIC solutions. However, some appear to
still be in the design and trial stage, and not yet finalized.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
AllRing: Bottom-up Analysis
We expect CoWoS-related revenue to account for 75% of AllRing's total revenue in 2026,
with AllRing focusing on oS equipment supply to TSMC and ASE/SPIL. However, we see
more non-TSMC 2.5 packaging solution vendors qualifying more Taiwanese CoWoS
equipment vendors, creating a new TAM for AllRing, including FoPLP and Intel's EMIB. We
expect AllRing's 2027 CoWoS revenue to rise 62% Y/Y (TSMC's CoWoS seen at 200kwpm

for 2027), and see it beginning to prepare equipment for CoPoS (if the trial round goes
smoothly in 2026) and its AP fab in Arizona (MP likely starting in 2028).
For CPO, AllRing has penetrated the CPO supply chain of NVIDIA for FAU-optical engine
coupling and FAU AOI equipment. Our latest supply chain checks suggest that AllRing may
have also won the dispensing step after insertion 3, where the optical engine will be
mounted onto the CPO package substrate. We expect more clarity from the company on

CPO-related revenue in 2H26, and expect it to account for 13%, 21%, and 28% of AllRing's
total revenue in 2026, 2027, and 2028, respectively.
For SoIC, we expect 2026 SoIC capacity to reach 14kwpm. TSMC further expanding its
CoWoS capacity in AP7 could cause slightly delays in equipment pull-in. Our latest supply
chain checks suggest that TSMC has revised 2027e capacity to 40kwpm (from 45kwpm)
and 2028e to 70kwpm (from 75kwpm). AllRing is the sole WoW dispenser supplier for

SoIC, but we expect the delay in SoIC to be more than offset by the strong build in
CoWoS. In the long run,  we still  expect it to be the key growth driver for AllRing. SoIC is
one of the key advanced packaging nodes for TSMC in the coming years, as we see more
and more customers adopting this technology to realize chiplet design, including AMD,
NVIDIA, Apple, and Broadcom.

AllRing: Valuation Methodology
We lift our RI-based price target (base case scenario value) from NT$1,280 to NT
$1,580: We factor in our estimate changes across 2026-28.  Our other key assumptions are
unchanged, including a cost of equity (CoE) at 8%, derived from a risk-free rate of 2.0%,
beta of 1.0 and market risk premium of 6%, intermediate growth rate of 16% and terminal
growth rate of 4.5%. Our bull and bear case values also rise, from NT$1,800 and NT$520

to NT$2,200 and NT$640, respectively, implying 56x and 16x our 2027 EPS estimate.

Risk Reward - AllRing Tech Co. (6187.TWO)
NT$1,580.00
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions are:Cost of equity (CoE) of 8%, derived from a risk-free rate of 2.0%, beta of 1.0, and a marketrisk premium of 6.0%.Intermediate growth rate of 16%.Cash dividend payout of 65%Terminal growth rate of 4.5%.
MS Rating
▪NVIDIA's next-generation GPU Rubin willstick with graphene for heat sinkattachment, reducing concerns aboutcompetition for Allring.▪We expect AllRing to be a key beneﬁciaryof the multi-year advanced packaging trend,including CoPoS and SoIC, but large-scalerevenue contributions will likely take time.▪It is the sole WoW (Wafer-on-Wafer)dispenser supplier for SoIC, which we expectto be the next growth driver, contributing atleast 2% of total revenue in 2026 and 4% in2027. ▪Our implied 2027e target P/E is 40x,similar to its SoIC peer and lower than CPOpeers. We expect new advanced packagingopportunities including CPO, CoWoP, andCoPoS to beneﬁt the company in the longrun.

NT$2,200.00
56x 2027e EPS
We assume: 1) 60% revenue CAGR over2025-28, thanks to stronger-than-expectedcapacity expansion plans for TSMC's CoWoS,CPO and SoIC; 2) gross margin expands tomore than 55% in 2025-28, due to stronger-than-expected operating leverage resultingfrom the equipment production outsourcingstrategy; 3) new product launches exceedexpectations and AllRing increases itsmarket share in other processes such as"CoW".

NT$1,580.00
40x 2027e EPS
We expect: 1) 44% revenue CAGR in 2025-28e after strong CoWoS capacity expansionfor TSMC in 2024-25; 2) gross margin to bemaintained at 48-53% from 2024-28 vs.48% in 2022 thanks to high operatingleverage resulting from the equipmentproduction outsourcing strategy; and 3) withnew equipment launches, we expect AllRingto expand its market share in otherprocesses. CPO, SoIC and other applicationscontinue to bear fruit.
NT$640.00

16x 2027e EPS
We assume: 1) -30% revenue CAGR in 2024-28, owing to slower-than-expected CoWoScapacity expansion plans by TSMC; 2) grossmargin falls below 40% owing to the highbargaining power of its customers; 3) marketshare loss in "WoS" process amid rising peercompetition.

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Asia Vital Components Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Takeaways from Asia AI Summit
2026
• AVC retains its 2026 outlook for sequential growth from both a revenue and
margin perspective. In 2027–28, AI ASIC projects will add incremental drivers
through shipment volume increases and high customization requirements.
Beyond 2028, China AI projects will see contribution growth as an additional
driver.
• AVC expects the new cold plate module project for the next-generation GPU

platform to begin volume shipments in 3Q26, while the new AI ASIC project
should start in late 3Q26 or early 4Q26.
• Two-phase liquid cooling represents a new thermal solution that AVC is
currently developing, targeting a 15% improvement in thermal performance.
Current tasks focus on pump design and air pressure management within
liquid loops.
• The capacity expansion plan this year aims to increase cold plate module
capacity by 5x. By end-2026, AVC targets a 50:50 capacity split between

China and Vietnam. The current plan for next year is to double the land area
at its Vietnam site.
• LEO satellite thermal solutions remain under development to meet high
reliability requirements across a wide operating temperature range.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Asia Vital Components Co. Ltd. (3017.TW)
Base case, derived from our multi-stage residual income (RI) valuation model, similar to the
rest of our tech hardware coverage universe. We assume a cost of equity of 10%, a medium-
term growth rate of 13%,                          and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected thermal upgrade trend for data centers, including server and

switch designs
n Accelerating 5G network deployment momentum
n Better-than-expected PC/NB demand or share gains
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected thermal design trend for data centers, including server and
switch designs
n Decelerating 5G network deployment momentum
n Intensifying industry competition

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Idea
1) Tesla AI chip  market share gets diluted by Samsung foundry: GUC stock fell
10% today (vs. Taiex -2%). Investors are concerned about potential competition/
share loss for GUC's existing projects/customers. Given leading-edge capacity
shortages and higher TSMC wafer costs, our recent industry checks show that the
AI5 packaging configuration could shift from two AI5 chips to one AI5 plus a co-
packaged backup AI4 built on Samsung 5nm. What’s more, both Samsung and Intel

foundries are likely to produce next-gen 2nm AI6 as we noted in our previous
Foundry Floorplan
. Although we have not yet baked any AI6 revenue into our
current GUC forecast, we think it might disappoint some bullish investors who
expect GUC to handle the majority of the next-gen Tesla AI project.
2) Google CPU projects could be shared by Samsung foundry as well:  Google may
also seek other foundry suppliers or potentially other ASIC partners for more

capacity. According to our industry checks,
Samsung's foundry is also likely to
produce next-gen Google CPU (Axion 3) from its SF2 (or 2nm) process, with yields
gradually improving.  We see potential market share loss for GUC’s major CPU
business in the coming years, offsetting Google CPU demand strength at GUC. We
believe we have baked some conservatism into contribution from GUC’s major
projects — including Google CPU (2mn units in 2026 and 2.5mn in 2027 and Tesla

AI5 (800k units in 2027 and 1.5mn in 2028 — (without AI6). Therefore, we make no
changes to our earnings forecasts. We believe potential competition should have
limited near-term impact. However, we don't see upside to our current earnings
forecasts or price target of NT$4,888 either.
Move GUC to EW to take profits until we see new project wins:  With the medium-
/long-term outlook affected and after the share price rally over the past two
months (+135% vs. Taiex +33%), we think it’s time to take profits, with P/E now at

50x our 2027 EPS. We could turn constructive again if 1) TSMC can allocate more
wafers to GUC with price discounts to regain market share, and/or 2) GUC wins new
major CSP projects.

### Page 2

Idea
2
Still strong chip demand from CPU  and AI business
How GUC  could participate in  TSMC's   automotive chip
foundry service
In TSMC: Thoughts about auto semi foundry dynamics; OW (1 Aug 2025), we noted that
the new AI5 will be manufactured using TSMC's 3nm process node and is expected to
deliver 4-5x higher performance than its predecessor, AI4. GUC could help in the design by
working closely with TSMC. We believe the Tesla AI project will only get bigger when the

new generation chip is introduced. For the  next-generation Tesla chip, despite the
competition from other foundries,  we still expect GUC to get involved in TSMC's version
of the AI chip too.
In the same report, we also noted that TSMC is the major autonomous driving chip
foundry for merchant chip suppliers such as Horizon Robotics and Nvidia (e.g., the Thor
chip).   We believe some of those merchant chips may go through a design service to ensure

time to market.
Some Chinese EV OEMs with custom ASIC chip designs are also potential customers for
Asian ASIC service providers and TSMC. For example, Alchip (OW) is starting the second
5nm ASIC for Li Auto at TSMC. XPeng is also seeking design service for its new generation
ASIC.
Exhibit 1: Tesla AI5
Source: Tesla
Strong Arm-based CPU revenue to dilute gross margin, but
still be positive for GUC's EPS

### Page 3

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
CPU turnkey revenue carries a low gross margin – likely in the single digits – because this
is a “Turnkey 2 or 3” project, where GUC provides advanced packaging design support (e.g.,
silicon interposers and ABF substrates) but does not own the photomasks for the ASIC
designs. Our checks  suggest that   Google CPU gross margin could also be 2-3ppts better
than our previous anticipation of 5% given the better order scale at TSMC. We now expect

strong Google CPU demand and already saw Axion CPU demand at least 7mn units from
2026-2028, generating close to US$5bn in revenue (ASP US$700) for GUC over the same
period.
GUC also helps with the Cobalt 200 CPU for Microsoft, an Arm-based design tailored for
Microsoft Cloud. Our checks also indicate strong demand for Cobalt at  >1mn units in 2027
and onwards. We therefore forecast US$500-600mn revenue contribution from this in
2027-2028.

Other major projects in 2026/2027
GUC has indicated that it will have three AI-related projects with US cloud service
providers (CSPs) active in 2026. We believe these CSP projects could account for over
50% of 2026e revenue and look for crypto-related momentum to continue at least

through 1H26, as the company has already secured foundry capacity support. In our view,
this underscores the importance of strong foundry relationships in obtaining scarce
leading-edge capacity. Including GUC’s other AI start-up engagements and IP licensing, we
estimate AI-related revenue will exceed 50% of total revenue in both 2026 and 2027.
• Microsoft: In our previous report, we indicated that GUC supports a Microsoft
3nm AI accelerator (Maia 200) TK2. Our industry checks suggest that Maia 200

tape-out should come in May 2026, with mass production later next year. GUC also
helps on the Cobalt 200 CPU, Arm-based design tailored for Microsoft Cloud.
• Amazon AI inference chips: GUC is helping AWS build the inference chip for AI
speakers, which could also start revenue contribution in 2026 and reach around
US$100mn in 2027.
• SanDisk and Kioxia SSD controllers:  From our industry checks, GUC has also been
working  with SanDisk on  SSD controllers, which should start to contribute some

NRE revenue in 2026, and then ~US$250mn in turnkey revenue in 2027.
• Chinese ADAS chipmaker:  In addition, GUC's auto customers are not limited to
Tesla only. A Chinese ADAS chipmaker  could also contribute revenue  by 2026 and
ramp in 2027.
• GLink IP licensing: In 2026, we also expect GUC to keep licensing its die-to-die
GLink IP to Google’s CPU project, which we estimate could generate US$3-5mn in
annualized revenue.

unchanged, including  cost of equity of 9.2% (a beta of 1.2, a risk premium of 6%, and a risk-

Relative valuation
GUC's share price has risen nearly 135% since April 2025 given the strong demand from
Google CPU and Tesla AI5 (the TAIEX is up 33% over the same period).
Our price target of NT$4,888 implies 48x our 2027 EPS estimate, lower than +1 SD from

GUC’s historical NTM P/E since 2013. We maintain our view that the design service market
will proliferate with AI ASIC outgrowing AI GPU in the long term. However, we think it’s
time  to take profits, with the stock now trading at 50x our 2027 EPS estimate with
potential competition.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
TSMC (2330.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, risk-
free rate of 2.0% and risk premium of 6.0%), an intermediate growth rate of 10.5%, and a
terminal growth rate of 4.0%.
Risks to Upside
n TSMC charges large customers more to keep its gross margin above 56% in the long
term.
n AI semi demand grows more significantly than expected, while TSMC maintains high

market share in leading-edge foundry business.
n Outsourcing from Intel CPU increases in 2025-27.
Risks to Downside
n Inventory correction occurs in 2026.
n Demand for leading edge technologies weakens.
n Costs of overseas fabs grow significantly.
Alchip Technologies Ltd (3661.TW)
Base case value, derived from a residual income model. We assume the cost of equity is con-
stant at 10.4% (2% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.4 beta). Our intermediate growth rate and

terminal growth rates are 16% and 5%, respectively.
Risks to Upside
n Demand for Chinese GPUs and AI chipsets takes off earlier than expected.
n NRE demand accelerates.
n Alchip wins more US hyperscaler HPC projects.
Risks to Downside
n China's HPC localization progress slows down.
n AI chipset progress is much slower to develop.
n NRE demand decelerates.
n Pricing competition intensifies.

STOCK RATINGS

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Yageo Corp.  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026 Feedback
• Distributor inventory remains healthy at <5 months (vs. slightly above 5
months in 1Q).
• In early stages of silicon capacitor development. Currently seeking partners
• Book-to-bill ratio is now at 1.3x.
• Not seeing any signs of consumer electronics demand slowdown in 2Q, but
visibility into 2H is limited currently.
• AI revenue was of the mix 15% in C1Q. Outside of tantalum and resistors,

magnetics contribute more to AI than MLCC.
• Typically, pricing negotiations with customers are  quarterly,but Yageo did not
comment on whether it expects to hike MLCC prices in 2H.
• Yageo adjusted prices for tantalum three times in the past 1.5 years; each
time it was for a different tantalum product.

Key Takeaways
There weren't a lot of pricing adjustments baked into 1Q revenue.
Thus,  there should be more (from the pricing adjustments made in tantalum,
resistors, magnetics, MLCC, etc.).
2Q revenue and margin likely have upside –  demand appears stronger than a few
weeks ago...
...with standard (75%+) and premium products (85%+) all running at higher

utilization vs. its guidance during the 1Q earnings call.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Yageo Corp. (2327.TW)
Base-case, multistage residual income valuation model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 7.8%
(risk-free rate 1%, equity risk premium 6.8% and beta of 1.1), medium-term growth rate 10%,
terminal growth rate 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected revenue/profit recognition from stronger AI server/notebook
demand
n Further price hikes led by stronger pricing power arising from better demand and/or

tighter supply
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected end-demand recovery
n Escalating US/China trade tensions
n Weaker automotive and industrial demand

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

徐力雯 
02-27815995#37111 
anny.hsu@fubon.com 
美食-KY (2723 TT)

中國消費復甦偏弱，2Q26 淡季表現承壓

維持買進，目標價下調至 73 元，潛在上漲空間 16%。 
1. 1Q26 店質調整帶動獲利改善，推升毛利率及營利率創新高。 
2. 美國 2026 年突破百店，2027 年展店節奏加速。 
3. 中國閉店節奏延續 2Q26 淡季營利率呈壓。

1Q26 店質調整帶動獲利改善 推升毛利率及營利率創新高 
1Q26 營收 40.3 億，YoY-16.7%，QoQ -7.1%，低於富邦/市場預期，主因美國地
區 1、2 月受暴風雪影響， 20%門市短暫停業，疊加中國市場持續關閉低效門店所
致 。 然受惠台灣與美國市場營運穩健及中國店質調整成效顯現，1Q26 毛利率 61.1%、
營利率 8.0%，分別 YoY+1.5/1.6ppts，營利率創 2020 年以來新高，整體獲利結構
顯著改善，優於富邦/市場預期，稅後淨利 2.4 億元，YoY+11.8%，EPS 1.31 元。

美國 2026 年突破百店 2027 年展店節奏加速 
1Q26 年集團區域營收占比：美國 55% 、大陸 26% 、及台灣 19%，美國持續為
集團第一大營運區域。美國截至 1Q26 店數 90 店，季增 2 店，然 SSSG 因暴風雪
影響低於 0-1%區間，OPM 維持 13%以上 。展望未來， 美國2Q26 預計再展 4 店，
2026 年底總店數有望突破 100 店，全年展店數超過 10 店；隨中央廚房及物流布
局逐步完善，未來供應體系已具備支應 200 店規模能力，預期 2027 年起展店速度
將進一步加快。在SSSG 維持穩健下，整體美國地區營收預期將維持雙位數年增(以
美元計算)，長期隨營運規模擴大與規模經濟效益顯現，毛利率與營業利益率仍具持
續提升空間。

中國閉店節奏延續 2Q26 淡季營利率呈壓 
中國市場延續 3Q25 經營調整策略、優化營運規模，依序退出獲利狀況低於預期區
域，店數、營收年比下降，截至 1Q26 店數降至低於 300 間(vs. 2025 311 店)，且
受惠汰弱留強單店月均營業額年比呈正成長。美食將持續透過直營轉加盟調整營運，
推估 2026 底總店數維持 300 店以下水準。展望 2Q26，受傳統淡季影響，中國市
場營利率承壓，隨 2H26 旺季中國有望轉虧為盈，逐步擺脫營運包袱。

業外支出大幅改善 上調 2026 獲利預估 
展望未來 ， 美國展店與營運動能延續 ， 台灣市場維持穩健 ， 中國市場持續調整體質 。
考量中國閉店損失屬一次性，預期 2026 年業外損失顯著改善，富邦上調 2026 年
稅後 EPS 預估至 4.87 元 (原預估 4.79 元)。

富邦不同之處 
富邦最新預估 2026、2027 年稅後EPS 為 4.87/5.66 元 ， 對比Bloomberg consensus
預估的 4.98/6.02 元。

評價與風險 
美食目前 PE 在 2026 年的 12-13x PER。考量美國營運持續樂觀，中國虧損有望損
平，2026 年集團營運重回成長，然 2Q26 淡季營利率呈壓，投資建議維持買進，目
標價下調至 73 元，2026 年 15xPER。主要風險為美國及中國營運未如預期，以及
原物料價格大幅上漲影響毛利率。

產業評等 定義 
優於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現突出 
持平 預估該產業在未來6個月內與大盤指數表現相較持平 
劣於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現較差

Fubon's Rating System 
Rating Definition 
Buy Expected absolute return to be over 15% within the next 6 months  
Neutral  Expected absolute return to be level within the next 6 months  
Sell  Expected absolute return to be over negative 15% within the next 6 months 
Not Rated (NR)  Pursuant to Fubon acting in deals involving the company or there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an investment rating  
Under Review Fubon is in the process of determining an investment rating and will assign a rating within the 3- to 6-month horizon

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

2026.05.22 
陳靖元 C00190@ibfs.com.tw 
3135.TT 凌航 
 產品報價迎補漲行情 2026 獲利強勁成長

[公司拜訪]   
   公司簡介 
凌航科技股份有限公司為動態隨機存取記憶體開發商。主要產品涵蓋 DRAM 模組、
Flash 快閃記憶體及 SSD 固態硬碟 。 公司具備強大的IC 測試技術與模組設計能力 ， 藉
由與上游原廠建立長期穩定的供應關係，為全球客戶提供一站式整合服務。  
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 260 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】無 
【大盤指數/股價】41368 / 195.5

➢ 1Q26 報價表現持續強勁，且產品組合逐漸優化 
1Q26 公司 DRAM 產品開始正式反映漲價後 ASP，同時出貨量方面，由
於適逢 2 月份春節假期，出貨天數減少，故預估位元出貨量將小幅季減
約 5%。1Q26 營收約 39.58 億元，QoQ+54.53%，YoY+177.96%，獲
利表現方面則受惠於報價漲勢強勁 ， 以及公司具相對低價庫存下 ，1Q26
毛利率提升至 30.35%，EPS 為 10.76 元，QoQ+149%，YoY+2,423%。 
➢ 2Q26 出貨開始反映報價強勁漲幅，獲利表現持續攀升 
考量到 1Q26 仍有部分客戶合約價尚未反映報價漲幅，凌航出貨反映報
價的時間應相對落後，故自 2Q26 全產品線才真正開始反應 DRAM 及
NAND 強勁漲幅 ， 預期2Q26 ASP 將維持強勁上行趨勢 ，DRAM 及 SSD 
ASP 預估分別季增逾 100% /150%，位元出貨量則大致與 1Q26 持平。
預估 2Q26 營收約 86.49 億元，QoQ+118.54%，YoY+397.37%，獲利
表現方面，受惠於 ASP 強勁漲幅以及產品組合優化，毛利率提升至
48.44%，稅後淨利為 31.2 億元，QoQ+264.53%，EPS 為 40.27 元。  
➢ 2026 年預計將迎來獲利噴發 
展望 2026 年，看好記憶體供需於短期不易緩解，市場將呈現賣方市場，
凌航客戶組成逐漸以工控 、 標案客戶為多數 ， 凌航得以轉嫁成本予客戶 ，
營收可望維持相對高檔，獲利則有望創新高。整體而言，預估凌航2026
年營收約 278.82 億元 ，YoY+261.91%， 毛利率為36.52%， 稅後淨利為
73.9 億元，YoY+1,557.4%，EPS 為 95.08 元。 
➢ 看好產業結構改變，公司朝工控發展，建議買進，目標價 260 元 
評價方面，參考過往淨值比區間約落在 1X~4X，預估 2026 年每股淨值
為 108 元，目前 PB 約 1.7X。看好公司客戶結構上的轉變，將有利於獲
利改善，故給予買進評等，目標價 260 元(2026F PB 2.4X)。投研部認為
目前記憶體需求仍處於快速上升階段，主要受惠於 AI 應用的大幅拓展，
因此產業需求出現結構性改變的情況下，將改變無論原廠或模組廠過往
景氣循環的獲利模式，預期隨著越多廠商簽訂 LTA，將逐漸改變市場對
於記憶體產業的評價模式，預期股價將迎來新一輪成長動能。

3135 凌航 加權指數

個股報告

公司簡介 
凌航科技股份有限公司創立於 1998 年，公司早期以 GSM 技術為基礎設計 SIM Card 
IC，並於 2007 年正式切入記憶體產業，轉型為動態隨機存取記憶體 DRAM 開發商。
目前資本額約為新台幣 7.75 億元 。 凌航科技定位為 「關鍵記憶體解決方案的製造商」 ，
主要產品涵蓋 DRAM 記憶體模組、Flash 快閃記憶體及 SSD 固態硬碟。公司具備強
大的 IC 測試技術與模組設計能力 ， 能針對不同終端應用提供客製化測試流程 ， 確保產
品在高效能與穩定性上達到國際品質標準 。 其營運模式高度聚焦於 B2B 與 B2G 市場 ，
藉由與上游原廠建立長期穩定的供應關係，為全球客戶提供一站式整合服務。  
凌航科技提供一站式記憶體整合服務 ， 其產品組合主要分為DRAM 記憶體模組 、Flash
快閃記憶體及 SSD 固態硬碟三大類。DRAM 產品：2025 年受惠於 DRAM 報價於
2Q25 開始上漲，故預估 DRAM 營收佔比將提高近八成，其中 DRAM 產品又涵蓋雲
端伺服器 RDIMM、標準型及領先同業推出的 LPCAMM2、CUDIMM 等新世代高階
產品。Flash 與 SSD：營收佔比約兩成，包含企業級 eSSD 與各種工業級存儲方案，
隨著市場對大容量存儲需求增加，營收貢獻度穩定提升。工控與利基型應用：公司積
極優化產品結構，將重心由消費型挪移至高附加價值的工控 IPC、邊緣 AI 及寬溫記憶
體方案  
圖 1：凌航產品營收組合

資料來源：公司, 國票投顧預估整理 
2025 受惠報價上漲，獲利顯著成長 
4Q25 記 憶 體 市 場 進 入 極 度 緊 缺 的 賣 方 格 局 ， 主 流 原 廠 如 美 光(MU.US) 、 三 星
(005930.KS)、SK 海力士(000660.KS)對通用型 DDR4/DDR5 的供貨採取限縮。凌航
營收組合主要為 DRAM 及 SSD，DRAM 自 3Q25 開始上漲 ， 而NAND Flash 自 4Q25
開始上漲，凌航 4Q25 出貨多為大幅漲價前的合約報價，故毛利率雖有在低價庫存的
幫助下提升，但並未能真實反映現貨報價漲幅。回顧 2025 年，隨著生成式 AI 帶動
HBM 與 DDR5 需求，產能排擠效應逐步發酵，公司策略性將庫存金額由 7 億元提升
至近 12 億元，以應對後續供給緊縮。進入2H25，記憶體合約價漲勢確立，公司憑藉
持有的低價庫存與漲價紅利，獲利動能顯著噴發。此外，工控產品營收佔比從 2024
年的 7.18%翻倍成長至超過 15%， 顯示出在市場缺貨時 ， 凌航具備快速切入利基市場
的競爭優勢。2025 年營收約 77 億元，YoY+40%，毛利率 10.04%，稅後淨利 4.46
億元，YoY+213%，EPS 為 6.33 元。

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
1Q26 報價表現持續強勁，且產品組合逐漸優化 
1Q26 公司 DRAM 產品開始正式反映漲價後 ASP，預估 1Q26 DRAM ASP 季增幅可
望逾 60%，同時由於 NAND 報價自 4Q25 開始調漲，預估 1Q26 SSD ASP 季增幅逾
4 成。出貨量方面，由於目前記憶體供需緊張，預期凌航將更加嚴格控貨，以維持健
康庫存水位，同時由於消費性產品對於價格波動較為敏感，導致電競產品佔比急遽縮
減，再加上 2 月份適逢春節假期 ， 出貨天數減少 ，故預估位元出貨量將小幅季減約 5%。
1Q26 營收約 39.58 億元，QoQ+54.53%，YoY+177.96%，獲利表現方面則受惠於
報 價 漲 勢 強 勁 ， 以 及 公 司 具 相 對 低 價 庫 存 下 ，1Q26 毛 利 率 提 升 至 30.35% ，
QoQ+11.24ppts ， YoY+25.31ppts ， 稅 後 淨 利 8.56 億元， QoQ+149% ，
YoY+2,423%，EPS 為 10.76 元。 
2Q26 出貨開始反映報價強勁漲幅，獲利表現持續攀升 
04/2026 營收約 38.53 億元 ，MoM+169.38%，YoY+607.83%， 營收大幅優於預期 ，
推測在出貨量方面，因受到物料交期拉長影響，加上 1Q26 工作天數較少，導致部分
訂單遞延至 4 月出貨，故 4 月出貨量有較顯著提升，預估 5 月、6 月出貨量將回復至
正常出貨水準 。 至於報價方面 ， 考量到1Q26 仍有部分客戶合約價尚未反映報價漲幅 ，
凌航出貨反映報價的時間應相對落後，故自 2Q26 全產品線才真正開始反應 DRAM
及 NAND 強勁漲幅，預期 2Q26 ASP 將維持強勁上行趨勢，DRAM 及 SSD ASP 預
估分別季增逾 100% /150%。此外，雖然前述表示 5 月、6 月出貨量將較 4 月下滑，
同時觀察對於價格波動較為敏感的消費/電競需求將轉趨疲弱 ， 然而看好工控 、 網通等
需求拉貨需求強勁，預期工控、網通營收占比將自 2Q26 開始顯著提升，進一步改善
獲利結構，預估 2Q26 位元出貨量大致與 1Q26 持平。整體而言，預估 2Q26 營收約
86.49 億元，QoQ+118.54%，YoY+397.37%，獲利表現方面，受惠於 ASP 強勁漲
幅以及產品組合優化，毛利率提升至 48.44%，QoQ+18.09ppts，YoY+44.84ppts，
稅後淨利為 31.2 億元，QoQ+264.53%，EPS 為 40.27 元。 
2026 年預計將迎來獲利噴發 
展望 2026 年，看好記憶體供需於短期不易緩解，市場將呈現賣方市場，模組廠由於
持有低價庫存，獲利將顯著成長，雖然凌航庫存水位相較於同業低，但考量到公司持
續積極確保貨源穩定，同時凌航客戶組成逐漸以工控、標案客戶為多數，客戶大多不
會因為價格上漲而降低購買數量，故凌航得以轉嫁成本予客戶，營收可望維持相對高
檔，獲利則有望創新高 。整 體 而 言 ， 預 估 凌 航2026 年營收約 278.82 億 元 ，
YoY+261.91% ， 毛 利 率 為 36.52% ， YoY+26.5ppts ，稅後淨利為 73.9 億 元 ，
YoY+1,557.4%，EPS 為 95.08 元。 
看好產業結構改變，且公司朝工控發展，建議買進，目標價 260 元 
整體而言 ，2025 年至 2026 年記憶體產業正處於結構轉變的階段 ， 受惠於三大原廠將
產能鎖定於 HBM 與伺服器 DDR5，導致傳統通路與工控市場出現嚴重的產能排擠，
使記憶體轉變為稀缺的戰略資源。凌航在過往記憶體市況處於下行階段之際，仍與原
廠維持良好關係，故看好凌航可望在貨源上保持相對穩定，同時透過嚴控產能，目前
確保庫存水位維持 3 個月左右。看好在目前市況下，公司會將業務重心從消費級挪移
至高附加價值的 AI 工控與邊緣運算領域 。 隨著產品驗證週期大幅縮短 ， 工控營收佔比
預計將顯著成長，進一步改善獲利結構。

評價方面，參考過往淨值比區間約落在 1X~4X，預估 2026 年每股淨值為 108 元，目

期公司將藉由這次的供需吃緊而與客戶簽訂 LTA，將有助中、長期營運獲利維持一定

無論原廠或模組廠過往景氣循環的獲利模式，預期隨著越多廠商簽訂 LTA，將逐漸改
變市場對於記憶體產業的評價模式，預期股價將迎來新一輪成長動能。

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
3303 岱稜(UNIVACCO)

報告日期： 2026/05/22 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 1Q26 年稅後 EPS 0.92 元 
岱稜公告 1Q26 營收金額 7.74 億元，YoY+3.91% ，QoQ+9.75% ，
營業利益 1.00 億元 ，YoY-25.66% ，QoQ+31.48% ， 稅後盈餘 0.86
億元， YoY-23.4% ，QoQ-11.67% ，稅後 EPS 0.92 元。

二. 預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.43 元 
岱稜 04/2026 營收金額 3.07 億元 ，YoY+18.44% ，MoM+14.21% 。
在美國地區需求回溫，加上稼動率提升與關稅下滑有助毛利率回
升，預估岱稜 2Q26 營收金額 8.82 億元， YoY+16.66% ，
QoQ+13.89% ，預估營業利益 1.59 億元， YoY+1.69% ，
QoQ+59.32% ，預估稅後盈餘 1.35 億元， YoY+95.01% ，
QoQ+56.12% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.43 元。

三. 推估北美地區 2026 年營收 15.6 億元 ，YoY+20.6% 
北美地區 2025 年營收金額 12.93 億元， YoY+13.6% 。預期 2026
年世足賽將於美國，加拿大，墨西哥聯合舉辦，有助北美地區相
關終端消費市場，加上美國對等關稅衝擊淡化，將有助岱稜美國
市場滲透率提升 ， 宏遠推估北美地區 2026 年營收金額 15.6 億元 ，
YoY+20.6% 。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 5.00 元 
岱稜 2026 年在波蘭廠貢獻全年度營運，另外 2026 年世足賽有助
美國地區終端消費市場，惟 2025 年業外處分利益墊高基期，岱稜
2026 年整體獲利呈現小幅成長 。 預估岱稜 2026 年營收金額 33.50
億元， YoY+11.41% ，預估營業利益 5.81 億元， YoY+24.34% ，預
估稅後盈餘 4.71 億元， YoY+9.85% ，預估稅後 EPS 5.00 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 60 元 
觀察岱稜歷史 PER 7~20X ，岱稜 2026 年 ROE 18% ，PER 9.8X ，
評價偏低，加上現金殖利率 6.1% 具下檔支撐，故投資建議買進，
目標價 60 元。 (12X 2026 年稅後 EPS )

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
岱稜科技成立於 1990 年，資本額 9.41 億元，並於 2007 年掛牌
上櫃。岱稜為全球領先真空蒸鍍與精密塗佈企業之列，研發與製
造多項燙金箔與光電產品，包含熱燙箔、冷燙箔、雷射箔、塑膠
燙金箔與保護膜、醫療膠帶、擴散膜等。燙金箔產品廣泛運用於
精緻印刷，應用遍及食、衣、住、行、育、樂，身為台灣第一家
上櫃的燙金箔公司，岱稜「 UNIVACCO 」的品牌名稱是岱稜引以
為傲的三大核心技術體現 ─UNIque chemical formula 獨特的化
學配方‧ VACuum metalizing 高規格的真空蒸鍍技術‧ Coating 
technology 精密的塗佈技術； Logo 的彩虹象徵了活力與創造，
如同彩虹的繽紛色彩 ， 期許 UNIVACCO 所研發與製造的燙金箔與
光電產品，能為人們增添色彩。

圖一、岱稜科技營運核心價值

資料來源：岱稜、宏遠投顧整理

三. 2026 年營運概況及展望  
岱稜真空蒸鍍薄膜俗稱「燙金箔」 ，燙金箔為在一定的溫度與壓力
下，將燙金箔轉印到承印物表面的加工材料。主要應用在食、衣、
住、行、育、樂各行業，產品包括酒瓶標籤、酒盒包裝、食品包
裝、食品/飲品標籤、菸盒/菸包、卡片海報各式印刷品、日月品/
日化品標籤與包裝、人造皮革書皮/封面、化粧品與香水包裝/標
籤、藥品包裝/標籤、服飾與布料、腳踏車架與陶瓷類產品。

真空蒸鍍薄膜產品主要有燙金膜、冷燙系列產品、貼合膜、裝飾
膜、商標膜、離型膜、轉印膜…等，多應用於民生工業。光電材
料產品主要包括電子膠帶、光學保護膜、製程保護膜等，其中製
程保護膜主要供應給台灣觸控面板廠使用。

全球燙金箔主要業者德國 Kurz 、Illinois Tool Works Inc. 、Kolon 
Corp、Crown Roll Leaf 與岱稜，其中德國 Kurz 為全球最大廠商，
市占率達 25~30% 。

圖二、燙金應用廣泛

資料來源：岱稜、宏遠投顧整理

岱稜真空蒸鍍薄膜 (VC) 產品主要有燙金膜、冷燙系列產品、貼合
膜、裝飾膜、商標膜、離型膜、轉印膜。光電材料產品 (OP) ，主
要有 SMD 封裝膠帶 、 電子膠帶 、 擴散膜 、 光學薄膜製程保護膜等。

圖三、岱稜產品

資料來源：岱稜、宏遠投顧整理

岱稜 2025 年營收金額 30.07 億元， YoY+0.47% 。岱稜 2025 年營
運重點包括： 1，美國地區雖然有關稅衝擊，岱稜己於 1Q25 拉高
美國地區庫存， 2Q25 營運衝擊有限， 3Q25 己開始向下游調整售
價，預期美國仍可小幅成長。 2，歐洲地區先前主要以經銷制度為
主，岱稜投資波蘭廠， 2Q~3Q25 正式營運，主要服務大型國際品
牌客戶，屆時將有助直接客戶比重提升。熱燙箔 2025 年營收金額
13.13 億元， YoY-1.65% ，冷燙箔 2025 年營收金額 15.5 億元，
YoY+2.92% 。

圖五、集團合併營收概況

資料來源：岱稜、宏遠投顧整理與預估

岱稜 01~04/2026 營收金額 10.82 億元， YoY+7.66% 。岱稜 2026
年營運策略包括： 1，2026 年世足賽將於美國，加拿大，墨西哥
聯合舉辦，有助北美地區相關終端消費市場，加上美國對等關稅
衝擊淡化，將有助岱稜美國市場滲透率提升，宏遠推估北美地區
2026 年營收金額 15.6 億元， YoY+20.6% 。2，岱稜波蘭廠，主要
服務大型國際品牌客戶，有助直接客戶比重提升，有助歐洲地區
營運成長。 3，在高單價雷射冷燙，營收比重持續提升，有助提升
毛利率。綜合上述，預估熱燙箔 2026 年營收金額 14.50 億元，
YoY+10.43%， 預估冷燙箔 2026 年營收金額 19 億元 ，YoY+22.6%。

岱稜 2026 年在波蘭廠貢獻全年度營運，另外 2026 年世足賽有助

公告配發現金股利 3 元，現金殖利率 6.1%

年股利政策，將配發現金股利 3 元，現金殖利率 6.1% 。

2Q26 預期 AEC 高階產品出貨逐步恢復，估 EPS 季增 35.8% 
貿聯 1Q26 毛利率季減 3.2ppts 至 28.8%，低於市場預期 3.9ppts，主因 2-3
月高階 AEC 出貨遞延，低階產品大幅提升，EPS 達 11.66 元，低於市場預
期 19.7%。展望 2Q26，本中心預期 AEC 出貨遞延僅屬客戶短期拉貨時程調
整，隨高階產品出貨恢復，HPC 毛利率有望逐步改善，公司預估 2026 年毛
利率維持於 31–33% 區間。本中心預估貿聯 2Q26 營收季增 8.7%至 226.7
億；毛利率估季增 3.3ppts 至 32.1%，較前次下調 1.8 個 ppts 主因低階產品
比重高於預期；EPS 15.83 元，較前次下調 7.9%，主因下修毛利率預估。 
HPC 持續受惠 Power 規格升級；工業與車用成長優於預期 
展望 2H26，隨機櫃功耗將由 GB200/300 的 120-150 kW 提升至 200kW 以
上，預估 VR200 機櫃產值可較 GB 系列提升 2-3 倍，而 2027-2028 年進入
將改採獨立 Power rack ，預估 Power whip 需求數量將提升 2-3 倍，預估貿
聯 HPC 業務 2026/2027 年營收分別年增 72%/64%。非 HPC 業務方面，工
業營收表現優於預期，成長來自工廠自動化復甦及半導體設備拉貨強勁，估
工業 2026/2027 年營收各分別年增 20%，較前次的 8%上調；車用同樣看見
復甦，動能來自 Tesla 電動車 BMS 線束，估車用 2026/2027 年營收分別年
增 9%/10%，較前次的年持平上調。 
重申貿聯買進評等，目標價 2,800 元 
此次下修貿聯 2026 年 EPS 6% ，主因 AEC 高階產品比重低於預期，下調毛
利率預估；上調 2027 年 EPS 7% ，主因工業及車用優於預期，上調營收預
估。我們認為貿聯股價已反映 1Q26 產品組合不利因素，並看好高階 AEC
拉貨動能將回升，隨 GPU 密度提升帶動機櫃短距互連需求，AEC 滲透率仍
具高成長空間，並逐步往 800G/1.6T 升級長期趨勢不變，因此維持貿聯買進
評等，以 2027 年 EPS 110.21 元、25 倍本益比，推得目標價 2,800 元。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
1Q26 EPS 低於元大/市場預期 19.1%/19.7%，主因毛利率不如預期；
2Q26 AEC 高階產品出貨逐步恢復，估 EPS 季增 35.8%至 15.83 元。 
⧫  
持續看好 HPC 受惠 Power 規格升級，隨 AEC 高階產品出貨恢復，毛
利率將逐步改善；工業與車用成長優於預期，上調 2027 年 EPS 7%。 
⧫  
AEC 滲透率仍具高成長空間，並逐步往 800G/1.6T 升級長期趨勢未
變，維持買進評等，目標價 2,800 元 (2027F EPS 110.21 元 x25PE)。

營運分析
1Q26 EPS 低於元大/市場預期 19.1%/19.7%，主因毛利率不如預期
貿聯 1Q26 營收 208.6 億元，季增 5.5%，其中 HPC 業務因新舊平台轉換影響動能減弱，而車用與
工廠自動化業務則呈現明顯復甦；毛利率季減 3.2ppts/年減 1.6ppts 至 28.8%，低於元大/市場預期
4.6ppts/3.9ppts，主因 HPC 業務毛利率低於預期；稅後獲利 22.7 億元，季減 17.0%，EPS 達
11.66 元，低於元大/市場預期 19.1%/19.7%。

2Q26 預期 AEC 高階產品出貨逐步恢復，估 EPS 季增 35.8%至 15.83 元
以產品結構拆分，1Q26 HPC 業務約占營收 46%，Power 與 Data 營收占比約各半，其中 AEC 被
歸類於 Data 業務，約占貿聯總營收 15%，可大致分為 100G/400G/800G 規格，速度越高毛利率越
佳，而 2-3 月高階 AEC 出貨遞延，100G 低階產品占比大幅提升，產品組合不佳使 1Q26 毛利率低
於預期。展望 2Q26，整體需求強勁趨勢不變，僅為客戶一次性拉貨時程調整， 預期隨高階產品出
貨恢復，HPC 毛利率有望逐步改善，2026 全年毛利率預估維持於 31–33% 區間。
預估貿聯 2Q26 營收季增 8.7%至 226.7 億；毛利率估季增 3.3ppts 至 32.1%，較前次下調 1.8 個
ppts 主因低階產品比重高於預期；營業利益估季增 34.7%至 41.9 億元；稅後獲利 30.9 億元，季增

35.8%，EPS 達 15.83 元，較前次下調 7.9%主因下修毛利率預估。

HPC 持續受惠 Power 規格升級；工業與車用成長優於預期 
貿聯的 Power 線束於 AWS、Microsoft 與 Nvidia GB/VR 等平台皆為主要供應商，展望 2H26，隨
機櫃功耗將由 GB200/300 的 120-150 kW 提升至 VR 系列的 200kW 以上，看好電源規格升級帶動
連接器產值提升，包括 1)Power Whip 預期由 60A 升級至 100A；2)Busbar 由 1400A 升級至
5000A，預估 VR200 機櫃產值可較 GB 系列提升 2-3 倍。而 2027-2028 年進入 Rubin Ultra 將改採
獨立 Power rack ，電壓升至 800V HVDC ，預估 Power whip 需求數量將提升 2-3 倍，預估貿聯
HPC 業務 2026/2027 年營收分別年增 72%/64%。  
非 HPC 業務方面，工業業務營收表現優於預期， 1Q26 呈現雙位數增長，主要來自工廠自動化復
甦及半導體設備拉貨強勁，估工業 2026/2027 年營收各分別年增 20%，較前次的 8%上調。車用業
務同樣看見復甦，動能來自 Tesla 電動車 BMS 線束，估車用 2026/2027 年營收分別年增 9%/10%，
較前次的年持平上調。電器業務仍未見明顯回升，預期隨 2H26 併購墨西哥家電後有望擴大營運規
模，估電器 2026/2027 年營收分別年減 3%/年增 11%。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電腦周邊 2026 年 5 月 26 日 BizLink 第4 頁，共 17頁  
2028 年 CPO 將進入大規模商業化時程，貿聯將切入光學解決方案 
貿聯 2026-2027 年成長動能來自 1)AEC 滲透率持續提升，估貿聯今明年 AEC 業務年增率>50%；
2)Power Whip/Busbar 產品隨電源規格升級帶動產值提升。2028 年預期 CPO 將進入大規模商業化
時程，CPO 架構下光連接元件之重要性顯著提升，貿聯積極布局光通訊領域，2026 年 1 月正式收
購新富生光電 XFS，產品包括光纖跳線、 MPO/MTP 高密度連接器與多芯光纖組件。同時亦與
SENKO 及 ficonTEC 攜手合作 FAU 關鍵模組，使其產品組合從既有的高速線材，有望進一步延伸
至 MPO 連接、Fiber Assembly 及光纖重排模組 shuffle box 等關鍵元件，貿聯預期 3Q26 於台南設
立光通產線，4Q26 有望初步貢獻，主要放量時間點為 2028 年。 
圖 4：新富生光電 XFS MPO Connector 圖 5：新富生光電 XFS 光纖配線產品圖

資料來源：GTC、元大投顧整理 資料來源：GTC、元大投顧整理 
預期 3Q26 於台南設立光通產線，看好與康寧合作之機會 
在 CPO 架構下，光模組被拆分為光引擎（OE）、光連接元件（如 FAU、MPO、Shuffle Box ）與
封裝等子系統，其中光連接元件的重要性顯著提升，成本占比可達三成以上，且為良率與性能關鍵，
帶動相關供應鏈商機浮現。 
其中 Shuffle Box 將千上萬條光纖進行高密度重新分配與匯聚，為 AI 資料中心關鍵的被動光學元件。
根據供應鏈調查，shuffle box ASP 估達 4000-6000 美金，一台 switch 約搭配 2 個 shuffle box，而
以 2027 年 scale out CPO 滲透率 12%假設，約隱含 13 萬個 shuffle box 需求，2027 年市場規模估
計達 200 億台幣，而隨 2028 年 CPO 進入大規模導入階段，預計市場規模將進一步倍增，本中心
看好 Shuffle Box 相關商機，具備 Shuffle Box 技術之台廠包含波若威及貿聯，看好光通題材有望帶
動貿聯評價走升。 
➢ 波若威  : 持續投入光纖配 線盒、 800G/1.6T 光纖套 件、客製化 Jumper 產品以及 MMC 
Jumper 等高密度光互連產品，估 2H26 進入量產階段。 
➢ 貿聯 : 收購新富生光電 XFS 後，亦同樣具備 Shuffle Box 生產能力，不排除與康寧合作之機
會，預期 3Q26 於台南設立光通產線，4Q26 有望初步貢獻，主要放量時間點為 2028 年。
